Examples of prohibited activity include illegal activity, weapons development, attacks on critical infrastructure, content that endangers children, fraud, election interference, and sexually explicit content. Certain high-risk uses (such as recommendations that affect an individual’s health, legal rights, finances, livelihood, or access to essential services) require additional controls, including a qualified human-in-the-loop and AI-involvement disclosure to end users.</p><p>We also require that Claude only be used in a <a href="/supported-countries">supported region</a>.</p></div></div></div></div><div data-orientation="vertical" data-hidden="" data-index="12" data-closed="" class="zftEOa_item"><h3 data-orientation="vertical" data-hidden="" data-index="12" data-closed=""><button type="button" data-value="" data-orientation="vertical" data-hidden="" data-index="12" tabindex="0" aria-disabled="false" aria-expanded="false" id="base-ui-_R_3ddbsnpfkq6btbH2_" class="zftEOa_trigger"><span class="tOQyXa_headline-6 zftEOa_question" data-component="Headline">Does Anthropic comply with the EU AI Act and other AI laws?</span><div class="zftEOa_icon" style="width:20px;height:20px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0"><svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M14.128 7.16482C14.3126 6.95983 14.6298 6.94336 14.835 7.12771C15.0402 7.31242 15.0567 7.62952 14.8721 7.83477L10.372 12.835L10.2939 12.9053C10.2093 12.9667 10.1063 13 9.99995 13C9.85833 12.9999 9.72264 12.9402 9.62788 12.835L5.12778 7.83477L5.0682 7.75273C4.95072 7.55225 4.98544 7.28926 5.16489 7.12771C5.34445 6.96617 5.60969 6.95939 5.79674 7.09744L5.87193 7.16482L9.99995 11.7519L14.128 7.16482Z"></path></svg></div></button></h3><div hidden="" data-orientation="vertical" data-index="12" data-closed="" id="base-ui-_R_3ddbsnpfkq6btbH1_" aria-labelledby="base-ui-_R_3ddbsnpfkq6btbH2_" role="region" class="zftEOa_panel" style="--accordion-panel-height: 261px; --accordion-panel-width: 838px;"><div class="zftEOa_answer"><div class="RMuY0W_root YQKUZG_wrapper" data-component="RichTextWrapper"><p>Yes. Anthropic monitors and engages with AI regulatory developments globally and is committed to meeting applicable obligations as they come into force. Our <a href="/transparency">Transparency Hub</a> contains a report on each of our models, with links to the full system cards and documentation on model capabilities, safety evaluations, and deployment decisions.
